- **writing-durable-markdown**: Use when creating or editing ANY markdown file - CLAUDE.md, a role preamble, a SKILL.md, a runbook, project state, State.md, a spec, a plan, a README, or docs. - **writing-for-agents-zh**: 给 Agent 写文档、写 SKILL.md、写 AGENTS.md / CLAUDE.md、写规则文件、让 Agent 每次跑都稳定。当用户说「怎么写一个好的 SKILL.md」「AGENTS.md 该写什么」「技能描述怎么写才会被触发」「文档太长 Agent 不看」「同一个技能每次表现不一样」「什么时候该拆成两个技能」时使用。核心概念：上下文指针（描述与规则行的措辞决定 Agent 何时去读）；两种负担（上下文负担 vs 认知负担）；信息层级（文件内步骤 &gt; 文件内参考 &gt; 披露到外部的参考）与渐进披露、同处放置、蔓延；每步的完成标准（清晰度与要求度、过早完成、后续步骤的拉力）；何时拆分（按序列、按调用方式）；引导词（用模型预训练里已有的紧凑概念锚定行为，正面表述优于禁止）；修剪（单一事实来源、环境即缓存、相关性、空操作句）。技能专属：模型调用 vs 用户调用、路由技能。也覆盖「改得更容易被正确触发」「别写成一本手册」「老是漏掉后面的步骤」这类说法。改编自 Matt Pocock 的 writing-for-agents（MIT）。 ([po-et/workbuddy-skills](https://github.com/po-et/workbuddy-skills/tree/HEAD/ported/skills/writing-for-agents-zh) · ★4)
- **writing-for-ai**: Write instructions an agent follows the same way every run, at the lowest load: SKILL.md files, subagent definitions and charters, the prompts a skill gives its subagents, CLAUDE.md and AGENTS.md, and slash commands. Use when creating, restructuring, tightening, or reviewing one. Not for one-line fixes, prompts written on the fly, or writing for people. ([alisterpineda/ai](https://github.com/alisterpineda/ai/tree/HEAD/plugins/core/skills/writing-for-ai))
- **writing-for-alignment**: Use when asked to write anything for other people to read about a problem, a piece of work or what to do next, and the request names no document type, or a decision memo, research synthesis or explainer ("write this up for the team", "put something together for the steering group on where the pilot leaves us"); when an author's draft is about to go to its readers; or when an author wants to work out their position first. Shape skills load it first. Not for slide decks, marketing or legal copy. ([jmaskell/writing-for-alignment](https://github.com/jmaskell/writing-for-alignment/tree/HEAD/skills/writing-for-alignment))
- **writing-for-astra**: Use when authoring or revising skills, AGENTS.md, or task instructions for GPT-6 Astra. ([TheAngryPit/TheAngrySkills](https://github.com/TheAngryPit/TheAngrySkills/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/writing-for-astra))
- **writing-for-audiences**: Use when writing prose a specific person or audience will read, act on, or hear — a report, a deck or speech, teaching material, an explanation, a message — and when a reader signals they did not understand ("what is this number of?", "explain it in simpler terms", "I don't get what you mean"). ([ViktorSoroka07/claude-delivery-skills](https://github.com/ViktorSoroka07/claude-delivery-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/writing-for-audiences))
- **writing-for-claude**: How to write instructions that work WITH Claude's design, not against it — skills, CLAUDE.md files, plans, system prompts, project instructions, or any context Claude will follow. Use when someone is writing a SKILL.md, drafting a CLAUDE.md, creating a plan, designing project instructions, writing system prompts, or reviewing any document that tells Claude how to behave. ([khglynn/HG-Skills-Public](https://github.com/khglynn/HG-Skills-Public/tree/HEAD/workflow/writing-for-claude))
